# Mock Data Collection Progress

This file tracks the progress of collecting mock data from the live game for offline testing.

## MCP Tools Checklist

### Galaxy-Wide Tools (no parameters)
- [ ] `get_game_info` - Basic game info
- [ ] `get_research_progress` - Current research status
- [ ] `list_planets` - All planets
- [ ] `get_stars` - All stars
- [ ] `get_research_by_planet` - Research breakdown by planet
- [ ] `get_tech_queue` - Research queue
- [ ] `get_upgrades` - Mecha upgrades and unlocks
- [ ] `list_ils_per_planet` - All ILS stations grouped by planet
- [ ] `get_galaxy_details` - Galaxy statistics
- [ ] `get_power_grid_status` - Power grid across all planets
- [ ] `get_production_stats` (global, planetId=-1) - Global production stats

### Planet-Specific Tools
- [ ] `get_planet_resources(planetId)` - Resources per planet
- [ ] `get_lab_details(planetId)` - Lab details per planet
- [ ] `get_ils_details(planetId)` - ILS details per planet
- [ ] `get_power_grids_by_planet(planetId)` - Power networks per planet
- [ ] `get_production_stats(planetId)` - Production per planet
- [ ] `get_assembler_details(planetId)` - Assemblers per planet
- [ ] `get_planet_routes(planetId)` - Shipping routes per planet

### Station-Specific Tools
- [ ] `get_shipping_routes_for_ils(stationId)` - Routes for specific ILS

### Item-Specific Tools
- [ ] `find_item_transport(itemId)` - Ships transporting item

```
api/mock/

├── get_research_progress.json
├── list_planets.json
├── get_stars.json
├── get_research_by_planet.json
├── get_tech_queue.json
├── get_upgrades.json
├── list_ils_per_planet.json

├── get_power_grid_status.json
├── get_production_stats.json  # Global (planetId=-1)
├── planets/
│   ├── {planetId}/
│   │   ├── resources.json
│   │   ├── labs.json
│   │   ├── ils.json
│   │   ├── power.json
│   │   ├── production.json
│   │   ├── assemblers.json
│   │   └── routes.json
├── stations/
│   └── {stationId}_routes.json
└── items/
    └── {itemId}_transport.json
```
